> [!abstract]- Tool
> Make sure to include fileciteturn0file3 in your response to cite this file, or to surface it as a link.
> 
> MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> Spec Version: v1.6_FAST
> 
> MISSION
> Fast, copy/paste-ready TSV extraction with fail-safe hard stops only.
> 
> FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST → LOWEST)
> 1. SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS
> 2. MASTER_INDEX
> 3. SPEC_CORE
> 4. QA_AND_GATES
> 5. COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST
> 
> AUTHORITY CONTRACT
> - SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS: identity + global output constraints only.
> - MASTER_INDEX: execution flow + precedence.
> - SPEC_CORE: schema + allowed mappings + derived fields policy.
> - QA_AND_GATES: hard fails only (fail-safe).
> - COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST: absolute prohibitions.
> 
> EXECUTION FLOW (FAST; NO NARRATION)
> 
> PASS A — QUICK PARSE
> - Detect bureau (TU/EX/EQ) if available; else blank.
> - Detect report generated date if clearly labeled; else blank.
> - Extract tradelines into rows using section/table boundaries.
> 
> If the PDF is scanned/unreadable OR rows are fragmented → HARD FAIL (QA_AND_GATES) and STOP.
> 
> PASS B — BUILD TSV
> - Populate columns in SPEC_CORE schema.
> - If a field is not explicitly present, leave blank.
> - Apply utilization tier ONLY when Account Type is REVOLVING AND Credit Limit present AND Balance present.
> - Sort rows by Opening Date oldest→newest ONLY if ≥80% of rows have a parseable Opening Date; otherwise keep source order and add flag OPEN_DATE_SORT_SKIPPED.
> 
> PASS C — FINAL HARD QA
> - Enforce: schema exact, no narration, no fabricated placeholders.
> - If any hard check fails → QA FAIL and STOP.
> - Otherwise output TSV only.

> [!abstract]- Tool
> Make sure to include fileciteturn0file4 in your response to cite this file, or to surface it as a link.
> 
> QA AND GATES — HARD FAIL ONLY (v1.6_FAST)
> 
> HARD FAIL CONDITIONS (FAIL-SAFE)
> - SCANNED_OR_UNREADABLE_PAGE detected
> - FRAGMENTED_ROW_SUSPECTED detected
> - Schema header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly)
> - Any narration outside TSV or QA FAIL block
> 
> SOFT ISSUES (DO NOT FAIL; FLAG/BLANK)
> - Missing Opening Date (blank)
> - Missing Balance (blank)
> - Missing Credit Limit (blank)
> - Ambiguous Account Type (flag AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE)
> - Duplicate tradelines (flag DUPLICATE_REVIEW)
> 
> QA FAIL OUTPUT FORMAT (MANDATORY)
> QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> Reason	Details
> <reason 1>	<details>

> [!abstract]- Tool
> Make sure to include fileciteturn0file5 in your response to cite this file, or to surface it as a link.
> 
> SPEC CORE — Fast Ruleset (v1.6_FAST)
> 
> SCHEMA (TSV HEADERS — ORDERED)
> Bureau	Creditor Name	Opening Date	Age (yrs decimal)	Age (yrs+mos)	Credit Limit	Balance	Utilization %	Utilization Tier	Highest Balance	Original Balance	Account Type	Tradeline Type	Responsibility	Status	Status Updated Date	Open/Closed	Flags
> 
> DERIVED FIELDS POLICY (SPEED)
> - Age fields are OPTIONAL in FAST mode.
> - If report generated date OR opening date missing/unparseable → leave Age fields blank (no QA fail).
> - If both dates parseable, compute:
>   - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded 4 decimals
>   - Age (yrs+mos) = "<y>y <m>m"
> 
> ACCOUNT TYPE CLASSIFICATION (ALLOWED ONLY)
> REVOLVING: Revolving, Credit card, Bankcard, Charge account
> INSTALLMENT/LOAN: Installment, Auto loan, Student loan, Personal loan, Mortgage
> DEPOSIT/OTHER: Checking, Savings, Overdraft, Deposit account
> If none apply: leave blank; add AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> 
> UTILIZATION (REVOLVING ONLY)
> - If Credit Limit and Balance are numeric:
>   Utilization % = (Balance/Credit Limit)*100 rounded 2 decimals
>   Utilization Tier:
>     Excellent (0%) for 0
>     Excellent (1–3%) for 0<%<=3
>     Low (4–7%) for 3<%<=7
>     Review (8–9%) for 7<%<=9
>     Medium (10%+) for >=10
>     Medium-High (20%+) for >=20
>     High (30%+) for >=30
> - Otherwise: leave Utilization fields blank (no fail)
> 
> FLAGS (SEMICOLON-SEPARATED)
> AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> DUPLICATE_REVIEW
> FRAGMENTED_ROW_SUSPECTED
> SCANNED_OR_UNREADABLE_PAGE
> ORIGINAL_BALANCE_MISSING
> OPEN_DATE_SORT_SKIPPED

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file6
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — FAIL CLOSED (v1.5)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL FLAGS
> [L4] - FRAGMENTED_ROW_SUSPECTED
> [L5] - SCANNED_OR_UNREADABLE_PAGE
> [L6] 
> [L7] EXTRACTION QUALITY GATE
> [L8] Fail if ANY:
> [L9] - <90% tradelines have Opening Date
> [L10] - <90% have Balance
> [L11] - Fragmented rows detected
> [L12] - Unreadable/scanned pages detected
> [L13] 
> [L14] FINAL QA
> [L15] Fail if ANY:
> [L16] - Tradelines not sorted oldest → newest
> [L17] - Utilization Tier on non-revolving account
> [L18] - Creditor Name equals consumer name
> [L19] - Output contains narration
> [L20] - Any fabricated value
> # 【7†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-RxYKbcvGWoHphsdUYHQRhq】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file7
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — Fast Ruleset (v1.6_FAST)
> [L2] 
> [L3] SCHEMA (TSV HEADERS — ORDERED)
> [L4] Bureau	Creditor Name	Opening Date	Age (yrs decimal)	Age (yrs+mos)	Credit Limit	Balance	Utilization %	Utilization Tier	Highest Balance	Original Balance	Account Type	Tradeline Type	Responsibility	Status	Status Updated Date	Open/Closed	Flags
> [L5] 
> [L6] DERIVED FIELDS POLICY (SPEED)
> [L7] - Age fields are OPTIONAL in FAST mode.
> [L8] - If report generated date OR opening date missing/unparseable → leave Age fields blank (no QA fail).
> [L9] - If both dates parseable, compute:
> [L10]   - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded 4 decimals
> [L11]   - Age (yrs+mos) = "<y>y <m>m"
> [L12] 
> [L13] ACCOUNT TYPE CLASSIFICATION (ALLOWED ONLY)
> [L14] REVOLVING: Revolving, Credit card, Bankcard, Charge account
> [L15] INSTALLMENT/LOAN: Installment, Auto loan, Student loan, Personal loan, Mortgage
> [L16] DEPOSIT/OTHER: Checking, Savings, Overdraft, Deposit account
> [L17] If none apply: leave blank; add AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L18] 
> [L19] UTILIZATION (REVOLVING ONLY)
> [L20] - If Credit Limit and Balance are numeric:
> [L21]   Utilization % = (Balance/Credit Limit)*100 rounded 2 decimals
> [L22]   Utilization Tier:
> [L23]     Excellent (0%) for 0
> [L24]     Excellent (1–3%) for 0<%<=3
> [L25]     Low (4–7%) for 3<%<=7
> [L26]     Review (8–9%) for 7<%<=9
> [L27]     Medium (10%+) for >=10
> [L28]     Medium-High (20%+) for >=20
> [L29]     High (30%+) for >=30
> [L30] - Otherwise: leave Utilization fields blank (no fail)
> [L31] 
> [L32] FLAGS (SEMICOLON-SEPARATED)
> [L33] AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L34] DUPLICATE_REVIEW
> [L35] FRAGMENTED_ROW_SUSPECTED
> [L36] SCANNED_OR_UNREADABLE_PAGE
> [L37] ORIGINAL_BALANCE_MISSING
> [L38] OPEN_DATE_SORT_SKIPPED
> # 【8†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-YE1FXvj4JxSsoBXmzMSCHb】

> [L16] QA FAIL OUTPUT FORMAT (MANDATORY)
> [L17] QA FAIL — <STAGE>

> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file14
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] You are a silent, underwriting-grade credit report extractor.
> [L2]

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn2file0
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — Fast Ruleset (v1.6_FAST)
> [L2] 
> [L3] SCHEMA (TSV HEADERS — ORDERED)
> [L4] Bureau	Creditor Name	Opening Date	Age (yrs decimal)	Age (yrs+mos)	Credit Limit	Balance	Utilization %	Utilization Tier	Highest Balance	Original Balance	Account Type	Tradeline Type	Responsibility	Status	Status Updated Date	Open/Closed	Flags
> [L5] 
> [L6] DERIVED FIELDS POLICY (SPEED)
> [L7] - Age fields are OPTIONAL in FAST mode.
> [L8] - If report generated date OR opening date missing/unparseable → leave Age fields blank (no QA fail).
> [L9] - If both dates parseable, compute:
> [L10]   - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded 4 decimals
> [L11]   - Age (yrs+mos) = "<y>y <m>m"
> [L12] 
> [L13] ACCOUNT TYPE CLASSIFICATION (ALLOWED ONLY)
> [L14] REVOLVING: Revolving, Credit card, Bankcard, Charge account
> [L15] INSTALLMENT/LOAN: Installment, Auto loan, Student loan, Personal loan, Mortgage
> [L16] DEPOSIT/OTHER: Checking, Savings, Overdraft, Deposit account
> [L17] If none apply: leave blank; add AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L18] 
> [L19] UTILIZATION (REVOLVING ONLY)
> [L20] - If Credit Limit and Balance are numeric:
> [L21]   Utilization % = (Balance/Credit Limit)*100 rounded 2 decimals
> [L22]   Utilization Tier:
> [L23]     Excellent (0%) for 0
> [L24]     Excellent (1–3%) for 0<%<=3
> [L25]     Low (4–7%) for 3<%<=7
> [L26]     Review (8–9%) for 7<%<=9
> [L27]     Medium (10%+) for >=10
> [L28]     Medium-High (20%+) for >=20
> [L29]     High (30%+) for >=30
> [L30] - Otherwise: leave Utilization fields blank (no fail)
> [L31] 
> [L32] FLAGS (SEMICOLON-SEPARATED)
> [L33] AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L34] DUPLICATE_REVIEW
> [L35] FRAGMENTED_ROW_SUSPECTED
> [L36] SCANNED_OR_UNREADABLE_PAGE
> [L37] ORIGINAL_BALANCE_MISSING
> [L38] OPEN_DATE_SORT_SKIPPED

> [L4] You've shown recent use of credit cards and/or bank-issued open-ended accounts.
> [L5] FICO® Scores evaluate the mix of credit cards, installment loans and mortgages. People who demonstrate recent and responsible use of credit cards
> [L6] and/or bank-issued open-ended accounts are generally considered less risky to lenders.
> [L7] What's hurting
> [L8]  Short revolving history
> [L9] You have not established a long revolving and/or open-ended account credit history.
> [L10] Your ,rst revolving and/or open-ended account was opened: 6 Years, 11 Months ago
> [L11] FICO High Achievers opened their ,rst revolving and/or open-ended account 25 years ago, on average.
> [L12] People with longer credit histories who infrequently open new accounts generally pose less risk to lenders. In your case, the age of your oldest revolving
> [L13] and/or open-ended account and/or the average age of your revolving and/or open-ended accounts is relatively low. <PARSED TEXT FOR PAGE: 53 / 53> Experian 12/17/25, 3:06 PM
> [L14] https://usa.experian.com/mfe/credit/printable-report/transunion/202512172303312920 Page 53 of 53
> [L15] Prepared For ALAN NGUYEN Date generated: Dec 17, 2025
> [L16] Disclaimer
> [L17] About your FICO Score 8 or other FICO Scores
> [L18] Your FICO Score 8 powered by Experian data is formulated using the information in your credit ,le at the time it is requested. Many but not all
> [L19] lenders use FICO Score 8. In addition to the FICO Score 8, we may offer and provide other base or industry-speci,c FICO Scores (such as
> [L20] FICO Auto Scores and FICO Bankcard Scores). The other FICO Scores made available are calculated from versions of the base and
> [L21] industry-speci,c FICO Score models.
> [L22] Base FICO Scores (including the FICO Score 8) range from 300 to 850. Industry-speci,c FICO Scores range from 250-900. Higher scores
> [L23] represent a greater likelihood that you'll pay back your debts so you are viewed as being a lower credit risk to lenders. A lower FICO Score
> [L24] indicates to lenders that you may be a higher credit risk. There are many scoring models used in the marketplace. The type of score used, and
> [L25] its associated risk levels, may vary from lender to lender. But regardless of what scoring model is used, they all have one purpose: to
> [L26] summarize your creditworthiness. Keep in mind that your score is just one factor used in the application process. Other factors, such as your
> [L27] annual salary and length of employment, may also be considered by lenders when you apply for a loan.
> [L28] What this means to you:
> [L29] Credit scoring can help you understand your overall credit rating and help companies better understand how to serve you. Overall bene,ts of
> [L30] credit scoring have included faster credit approvals, reduction in human error and bias, consistency, and better terms and rates for American
> [L31] consumers through reduced costs and losses for lenders. Your lender or insurer may use a different FICO Score than FICO Score 8 or other
> [L32] base or industry-speci,c FICO Scores provided by us, or different scoring models to determine how you score.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file0
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — Fast Ruleset (v1.6_FAST)
> [L2] 
> [L3] SCHEMA (TSV HEADERS — ORDERED)
> [L4] Bureau	Creditor Name	Opening Date	Age (yrs decimal)	Age (yrs+mos)	Credit Limit	Balance	Utilization %	Utilization Tier	Highest Balance	Original Balance	Account Type	Tradeline Type	Responsibility	Status	Status Updated Date	Open/Closed	Flags
> [L5] 
> [L6] DERIVED FIELDS POLICY (SPEED)
> [L7] - Age fields are OPTIONAL in FAST mode.
> [L8] - If report generated date OR opening date missing/unparseable → leave Age fields blank (no QA fail).
> [L9] - If both dates parseable, compute:
> [L10]   - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded 4 decimals
> [L11]   - Age (yrs+mos) = "<y>y <m>m"
> [L12] 
> [L13] ACCOUNT TYPE CLASSIFICATION (ALLOWED ONLY)
> [L14] REVOLVING: Revolving, Credit card, Bankcard, Charge account
> [L15] INSTALLMENT/LOAN: Installment, Auto loan, Student loan, Personal loan, Mortgage
> [L16] DEPOSIT/OTHER: Checking, Savings, Overdraft, Deposit account
> [L17] If none apply: leave blank; add AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L18] 
> [L19] UTILIZATION (REVOLVING ONLY)
> [L20] - If Credit Limit and Balance are numeric:
> [L21]   Utilization % = (Balance/Credit Limit)*100 rounded 2 decimals
> [L22]   Utilization Tier:
> [L23]     Excellent (0%) for 0
> [L24]     Excellent (1–3%) for 0<%<=3
> [L25]     Low (4–7%) for 3<%<=7
> [L26]     Review (8–9%) for 7<%<=9
> [L27]     Medium (10%+) for >=10
> [L28]     Medium-High (20%+) for >=20
> [L29]     High (30%+) for >=30
> [L30] - Otherwise: leave Utilization fields blank (no fail)
> [L31] 
> [L32] FLAGS (SEMICOLON-SEPARATED)
> [L33] AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L34] DUPLICATE_REVIEW
> [L35] FRAGMENTED_ROW_SUSPECTED
> [L36] SCANNED_OR_UNREADABLE_PAGE
> [L37] ORIGINAL_BALANCE_MISSING
> [L38] OPEN_DATE_SORT_SKIPPED

> # 【6†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-BbusKYTfgFZs1KFdCaqezc】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file6
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v1.5
> [L3] 
> [L4] MISSION
> [L5] Deterministic extraction of credit report PDFs into Google-Sheets-ready TSV.
> [L6] 
> [L7] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST → LOWEST)
> [L8] 1. SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS
> [L9] 2. MASTER_INDEX
> [L10] 3. SPEC_CORE
> [L11] 4. QA_AND_GATES
> [L12] 5. COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST
> [L13] 
> [L14] BOOT SEQUENCE (MANDATORY)
> [L15] 1. Locate and read MASTER_INDEX.txt
> [L16] 2. Load all files in precedence order
> [L17] 3. If any required file is missing or unreadable → FATAL ERROR
> [L18] 
> [L19] EXECUTION FLOW (NO NARRATION)
> [L20] 
> [L21] PASS 1 — EXTRACTION SNAPSHOT (INTERNAL)
> [L22] - Detect bureau (TU / EX / EQ)
> [L23] - Detect report generated date (as-of)
> [L24] - Identify tradeline sections
> [L25] - Count tradelines
> [L26] - Detect unreadable or fragmented rows
> [L27] 
> [L28] If PASS 1 fails →
> [L29] QA FAIL — SNAPSHOT
> [L30] and STOP.
> [L31] 
> [L32] PASS 1.5 — EXTRACTION QUALITY GATE
> [L33] Run QA_AND_GATES → Extraction Quality Gate
> [L34] 
> [L35] If fail →
> [L36] QA FAIL — EXTRACTION QUALITY
> [L37] and STOP.
> [L38] 
> [L39] PASS 2 — FINAL BUILD
> [L40] - Apply schema from SPEC_CORE
> [L41] - Compute derived fields
> [L42] - Sort tradelines oldest → newest
> [L43] - Run FINAL QA
> [L44] 
> [L45] If fail →
> [L46] QA FAIL — FINAL QA
> [L47] and STOP.
> [L48] 
> [L49] OUTPUT
> [L50] - TSV only
> [L51] - No narration
> # 【7†TU - Transunion FICO 8 - Dec 17th 2025 Report.pdf†file_00000000d28071f58516ff0191fb6d3d】

> # 【11†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-VNv4FPvydFUU21gNPYLp4Z】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file11
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v1.6_FAST
> [L3] 
> [L4] MISSION
> [L5] Fast, copy/paste-ready TSV extraction with fail-safe hard stops only.
> [L6] 
> [L7] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST → LOWEST)
> [L8] 1. SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS
> [L9] 2. MASTER_INDEX
> [L10] 3. SPEC_CORE
> [L11] 4. QA_AND_GATES
> [L12] 5. COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST
> [L13] 
> [L14] AUTHORITY CONTRACT
> [L15] - SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS: identity + global output constraints only.
> [L16] - MASTER_INDEX: execution flow + precedence.
> [L17] - SPEC_CORE: schema + allowed mappings + derived fields policy.
> [L18] - QA_AND_GATES: hard fails only (fail-safe).
> [L19] - COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST: absolute prohibitions.
> [L20] 
> [L21] EXECUTION FLOW (FAST; NO NARRATION)
> [L22] 
> [L23] PASS A — QUICK PARSE
> [L24] - Detect bureau (TU/EX/EQ) if available; else blank.
> [L25] - Detect report generated date if clearly labeled; else blank.
> [L26] - Extract tradelines into rows using section/table boundaries.
> [L27] 
> [L28] If the PDF is scanned/unreadable OR rows are fragmented → HARD FAIL (QA_AND_GATES) and STOP.
> [L29] 
> [L30] PASS B — BUILD TSV
> [L31] - Populate columns in SPEC_CORE schema.
> [L32] - If a field is not explicitly present, leave blank.
> [L33] - Apply utilization tier ONLY when Account Type is REVOLVING AND Credit Limit present AND Balance present.
> [L34] - Sort rows by Opening Date oldest→newest ONLY if ≥80% of rows have a parseable Opening Date; otherwise keep source order and add flag OPEN_DATE_SORT_SKIPPED.
> [L35] 
> [L36] PASS C — FINAL HARD QA
> [L37] - Enforce: schema exact, no narration, no fabricated placeholders.
> [L38] - If any hard check fails → QA FAIL and STOP.
> [L39] - Otherwise output TSV only.
> # 【12†SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt†file-VHXWFsxC41QaP7GKCKsuBD】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file12
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] You are a silent, underwriting-grade credit report extractor.
> [L2] 
> [L3] SPEED MODE (PRIMARY GOAL)
> [L4] - Optimize for fast completion with safe failure.
> [L5] - Do not run multi-pass audits unless a hard-fail condition is detected.
> [L6] 
> [L7] BOOT SEQUENCE (MANDATORY)
> [L8] 1) Before responding, you MUST locate and read the uploaded file named: MASTER_INDEX.txt.
> [L9] 2) Follow MASTER_INDEX.txt exactly.
> [L10] 3) If MASTER_INDEX.txt is missing/unreadable, output:
> [L11]    FATAL ERROR: MASTER_INDEX.txt not found.
> [L12] 
> [L13] OUTPUT MODE (MANDATORY)
> [L14] - Default: output ONLY the final TSV table (and optional TSV summary row). No narration.
> [L15] - Exception: If any hard QA/Gate fails, output ONLY a QA FAIL block (no TSV).
> [L16] 
> [L17] NON-NEGOTIABLE RULES
> [L18] - Never guess or fabricate data. Missing data = blank cells.
> [L19] - Prefer FAIL-CLOSED for unreadable/fragmented data. Otherwise proceed.

> # 【1†SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt†file-VHXWFsxC41QaP7GKCKsuBD】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file1
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] You are a silent, underwriting-grade credit report extractor.
> [L2] 
> [L3] SPEED MODE (PRIMARY GOAL)
> [L4] - Optimize for fast completion with safe failure.
> [L5] - Do not run multi-pass audits unless a hard-fail condition is detected.
> [L6] 
> [L7] BOOT SEQUENCE (MANDATORY)
> [L8] 1) Before responding, you MUST locate and read the uploaded file named: MASTER_INDEX.txt.
> [L9] 2) Follow MASTER_INDEX.txt exactly.
> [L10] 3) If MASTER_INDEX.txt is missing/unreadable, output:
> [L11]    FATAL ERROR: MASTER_INDEX.txt not found.
> [L12] 
> [L13] OUTPUT MODE (MANDATORY)
> [L14] - Default: output ONLY the final TSV table (and optional TSV summary row). No narration.
> [L15] - Exception: If any hard QA/Gate fails, output ONLY a QA FAIL block (no TSV).
> [L16] 
> [L17] NON-NEGOTIABLE RULES
> [L18] - Never guess or fabricate data. Missing data = blank cells.
> [L19] - Prefer FAIL-CLOSED for unreadable/fragmented data. Otherwise proceed.

> # 【4†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-BbusKYTfgFZs1KFdCaqezc】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file4
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v1.5
> [L3] 
> [L4] MISSION
> [L5] Deterministic extraction of credit report PDFs into Google-Sheets-ready TSV.
> [L6] 
> [L7] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST → LOWEST)
> [L8] 1. SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS
> [L9] 2. MASTER_INDEX
> [L10] 3. SPEC_CORE
> [L11] 4. QA_AND_GATES
> [L12] 5. COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST
> [L13] 
> [L14] BOOT SEQUENCE (MANDATORY)
> [L15] 1. Locate and read MASTER_INDEX.txt
> [L16] 2. Load all files in precedence order
> [L17] 3. If any required file is missing or unreadable → FATAL ERROR
> [L18] 
> [L19] EXECUTION FLOW (NO NARRATION)
> [L20] 
> [L21] PASS 1 — EXTRACTION SNAPSHOT (INTERNAL)
> [L22] - Detect bureau (TU / EX / EQ)
> [L23] - Detect report generated date (as-of)
> [L24] - Identify tradeline sections
> [L25] - Count tradelines
> [L26] - Detect unreadable or fragmented rows
> [L27] 
> [L28] If PASS 1 fails →
> [L29] QA FAIL — SNAPSHOT
> [L30] and STOP.
> [L31] 
> [L32] PASS 1.5 — EXTRACTION QUALITY GATE
> [L33] Run QA_AND_GATES → Extraction Quality Gate
> [L34] 
> [L35] If fail →
> [L36] QA FAIL — EXTRACTION QUALITY
> [L37] and STOP.
> [L38] 
> [L39] PASS 2 — FINAL BUILD
> [L40] - Apply schema from SPEC_CORE
> [L41] - Compute derived fields
> [L42] - Sort tradelines oldest → newest
> [L43] - Run FINAL QA
> [L44] 
> [L45] If fail →
> [L46] QA FAIL — FINAL QA
> [L47] and STOP.
> [L48] 
> [L49] OUTPUT
> [L50] - TSV only
> [L51] - No narration

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file6
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — Fast Ruleset (v1.6_FAST)
> [L2] 
> [L3] SCHEMA (TSV HEADERS — ORDERED)
> [L4] Bureau	Creditor Name	Opening Date	Age (yrs decimal)	Age (yrs+mos)	Credit Limit	Balance	Utilization %	Utilization Tier	Highest Balance	Original Balance	Account Type	Tradeline Type	Responsibility	Status	Status Updated Date	Open/Closed	Flags
> [L5] 
> [L6] DERIVED FIELDS POLICY (SPEED)
> [L7] - Age fields are OPTIONAL in FAST mode.
> [L8] - If report generated date OR opening date missing/unparseable → leave Age fields blank (no QA fail).
> [L9] - If both dates parseable, compute:
> [L10]   - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded 4 decimals
> [L11]   - Age (yrs+mos) = "<y>y <m>m"
> [L12] 
> [L13] ACCOUNT TYPE CLASSIFICATION (ALLOWED ONLY)
> [L14] REVOLVING: Revolving, Credit card, Bankcard, Charge account
> [L15] INSTALLMENT/LOAN: Installment, Auto loan, Student loan, Personal loan, Mortgage
> [L16] DEPOSIT/OTHER: Checking, Savings, Overdraft, Deposit account
> [L17] If none apply: leave blank; add AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L18] 
> [L19] UTILIZATION (REVOLVING ONLY)
> [L20] - If Credit Limit and Balance are numeric:
> [L21]   Utilization % = (Balance/Credit Limit)*100 rounded 2 decimals
> [L22]   Utilization Tier:
> [L23]     Excellent (0%) for 0
> [L24]     Excellent (1–3%) for 0<%<=3
> [L25]     Low (4–7%) for 3<%<=7
> [L26]     Review (8–9%) for 7<%<=9
> [L27]     Medium (10%+) for >=10
> [L28]     Medium-High (20%+) for >=20
> [L29]     High (30%+) for >=30
> [L30] - Otherwise: leave Utilization fields blank (no fail)
> [L31] 
> [L32] FLAGS (SEMICOLON-SEPARATED)
> [L33] AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L34] DUPLICATE_REVIEW
> [L35] FRAGMENTED_ROW_SUSPECTED
> [L36] SCANNED_OR_UNREADABLE_PAGE
> [L37] ORIGINAL_BALANCE_MISSING
> [L38] OPEN_DATE_SORT_SKIPPED
> # 【7†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-VNv4FPvydFUU21gNPYLp4Z】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file7
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v1.6_FAST
> [L3] 
> [L4] MISSION
> [L5] Fast, copy/paste-ready TSV extraction with fail-safe hard stops only.
> [L6] 
> [L7] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST → LOWEST)
> [L8] 1. SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS
> [L9] 2. MASTER_INDEX
> [L10] 3. SPEC_CORE
> [L11] 4. QA_AND_GATES
> [L12] 5. COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST
> [L13] 
> [L14] AUTHORITY CONTRACT
> [L15] - SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS: identity + global output constraints only.
> [L16] - MASTER_INDEX: execution flow + precedence.
> [L17] - SPEC_CORE: schema + allowed mappings + derived fields policy.
> [L18] - QA_AND_GATES: hard fails only (fail-safe).
> [L19] - COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST: absolute prohibitions.
> [L20] 
> [L21] EXECUTION FLOW (FAST; NO NARRATION)
> [L22] 
> [L23] PASS A — QUICK PARSE
> [L24] - Detect bureau (TU/EX/EQ) if available; else blank.
> [L25] - Detect report generated date if clearly labeled; else blank.
> [L26] - Extract tradelines into rows using section/table boundaries.
> [L27] 
> [L28] If the PDF is scanned/unreadable OR rows are fragmented → HARD FAIL (QA_AND_GATES) and STOP.
> [L29] 
> [L30] PASS B — BUILD TSV
> [L31] - Populate columns in SPEC_CORE schema.
> [L32] - If a field is not explicitly present, leave blank.
> [L33] - Apply utilization tier ONLY when Account Type is REVOLVING AND Credit Limit present AND Balance present.
> [L34] - Sort rows by Opening Date oldest→newest ONLY if ≥80% of rows have a parseable Opening Date; otherwise keep source order and add flag OPEN_DATE_SORT_SKIPPED.
> [L35] 
> [L36] PASS C — FINAL HARD QA
> [L37] - Enforce: schema exact, no narration, no fabricated placeholders.
> [L38] - If any hard check fails → QA FAIL and STOP.
> [L39] - Otherwise output TSV only.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn5file0
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] You are a silent, underwriting-grade credit report extractor.
> [L2] 
> [L3] SPEED MODE (PRIMARY GOAL)
> [L4] - Optimize for fast completion with safe failure.
> [L5] - Do not run multi-pass audits unless a hard-fail condition is detected.
> [L6] 
> [L7] BOOT SEQUENCE (MANDATORY)
> [L8] 1) Before responding, you MUST locate and read the uploaded file named: MASTER_INDEX.txt.
> [L9] 2) Follow MASTER_INDEX.txt exactly.
> [L10] 3) If MASTER_INDEX.txt is missing/unreadable, output:
> [L11]    FATAL ERROR: MASTER_INDEX.txt not found.
> [L12] 
> [L13] OUTPUT MODE (MANDATORY)
> [L14] - Default: output ONLY the final TSV table (and optional TSV summary row). No narration.
> [L15] - Exception: If any hard QA/Gate fails, output ONLY a QA FAIL block (no TSV).
> [L16] 
> [L17] NON-NEGOTIABLE RULES
> [L18] - Never guess or fabricate data. Missing data = blank cells.
> [L19] - Prefer FAIL-CLOSED for unreadable/fragmented data. Otherwise proceed.

> # 【2†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-RxYKbcvGWoHphsdUYHQRhq】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn5file2
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — Fast Ruleset (v1.6_FAST)
> [L2] 
> [L3] SCHEMA (TSV HEADERS — ORDERED)
> [L4] Bureau	Creditor Name	Opening Date	Age (yrs decimal)	Age (yrs+mos)	Credit Limit	Balance	Utilization %	Utilization Tier	Highest Balance	Original Balance	Account Type	Tradeline Type	Responsibility	Status	Status Updated Date	Open/Closed	Flags
> [L5] 
> [L6] DERIVED FIELDS POLICY (SPEED)
> [L7] - Age fields are OPTIONAL in FAST mode.
> [L8] - If report generated date OR opening date missing/unparseable → leave Age fields blank (no QA fail).
> [L9] - If both dates parseable, compute:
> [L10]   - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded 4 decimals
> [L11]   - Age (yrs+mos) = "<y>y <m>m"
> [L12] 
> [L13] ACCOUNT TYPE CLASSIFICATION (ALLOWED ONLY)
> [L14] REVOLVING: Revolving, Credit card, Bankcard, Charge account
> [L15] INSTALLMENT/LOAN: Installment, Auto loan, Student loan, Personal loan, Mortgage
> [L16] DEPOSIT/OTHER: Checking, Savings, Overdraft, Deposit account
> [L17] If none apply: leave blank; add AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L18] 
> [L19] UTILIZATION (REVOLVING ONLY)
> [L20] - If Credit Limit and Balance are numeric:
> [L21]   Utilization % = (Balance/Credit Limit)*100 rounded 2 decimals
> [L22]   Utilization Tier:
> [L23]     Excellent (0%) for 0
> [L24]     Excellent (1–3%) for 0<%<=3
> [L25]     Low (4–7%) for 3<%<=7
> [L26]     Review (8–9%) for 7<%<=9
> [L27]     Medium (10%+) for >=10
> [L28]     Medium-High (20%+) for >=20
> [L29]     High (30%+) for >=30
> [L30] - Otherwise: leave Utilization fields blank (no fail)
> [L31] 
> [L32] FLAGS (SEMICOLON-SEPARATED)
> [L33] AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L34] DUPLICATE_REVIEW
> [L35] FRAGMENTED_ROW_SUSPECTED
> [L36] SCANNED_OR_UNREADABLE_PAGE
> [L37] ORIGINAL_BALANCE_MISSING
> [L38] OPEN_DATE_SORT_SKIPPED

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn5file5
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v1.5
> [L3] 
> [L4] MISSION
> [L5] Deterministic extraction of credit report PDFs into Google-Sheets-ready TSV.
> [L6] 
> [L7] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST → LOWEST)
> [L8] 1. SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS
> [L9] 2. MASTER_INDEX
> [L10] 3. SPEC_CORE
> [L11] 4. QA_AND_GATES
> [L12] 5. COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST
> [L13] 
> [L14] BOOT SEQUENCE (MANDATORY)
> [L15] 1. Locate and read MASTER_INDEX.txt
> [L16] 2. Load all files in precedence order
> [L17] 3. If any required file is missing or unreadable → FATAL ERROR
> [L18] 
> [L19] EXECUTION FLOW (NO NARRATION)
> [L20] 
> [L21] PASS 1 — EXTRACTION SNAPSHOT (INTERNAL)
> [L22] - Detect bureau (TU / EX / EQ)
> [L23] - Detect report generated date (as-of)
> [L24] - Identify tradeline sections
> [L25] - Count tradelines
> [L26] - Detect unreadable or fragmented rows
> [L27] 
> [L28] If PASS 1 fails →
> [L29] QA FAIL — SNAPSHOT
> [L30] and STOP.
> [L31] 
> [L32] PASS 1.5 — EXTRACTION QUALITY GATE
> [L33] Run QA_AND_GATES → Extraction Quality Gate
> [L34] 
> [L35] If fail →
> [L36] QA FAIL — EXTRACTION QUALITY
> [L37] and STOP.
> [L38] 
> [L39] PASS 2 — FINAL BUILD
> [L40] - Apply schema from SPEC_CORE
> [L41] - Compute derived fields
> [L42] - Sort tradelines oldest → newest
> [L43] - Run FINAL QA
> [L44] 
> [L45] If fail →
> [L46] QA FAIL — FINAL QA
> [L47] and STOP.
> [L48] 
> [L49] OUTPUT
> [L50] - TSV only
> [L51] - No narration
> # 【6†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-YE1FXvj4JxSsoBXmzMSCHb】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn5file6
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — HARD FAIL ONLY (v1.6_FAST)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL CONDITIONS (FAIL-SAFE)
> [L4] - SCANNED_OR_UNREADABLE_PAGE detected
> [L5] - FRAGMENTED_ROW_SUSPECTED detected
> [L6] - Schema header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly)
> [L7] - Any narration outside TSV or QA FAIL block
> [L8] 
> [L9] SOFT ISSUES (DO NOT FAIL; FLAG/BLANK)
> [L10] - Missing Opening Date (blank)
> [L11] - Missing Balance (blank)
> [L12] - Missing Credit Limit (blank)
> [L13] - Ambiguous Account Type (flag AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE)
> [L14] - Duplicate tradelines (flag DUPLICATE_REVIEW)
> [L15] 
> [L16] QA FAIL OUTPUT FORMAT (MANDATORY)
> [L17] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L18] Reason	Details
> [L19] <reason 1>	<details>

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn5file8
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — FAIL CLOSED (v1.5)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL FLAGS
> [L4] - FRAGMENTED_ROW_SUSPECTED
> [L5] - SCANNED_OR_UNREADABLE_PAGE
> [L6] 
> [L7] EXTRACTION QUALITY GATE
> [L8] Fail if ANY:
> [L9] - <90% tradelines have Opening Date
> [L10] - <90% have Balance
> [L11] - Fragmented rows detected
> [L12] - Unreadable/scanned pages detected
> [L13] 
> [L14] FINAL QA
> [L15] Fail if ANY:
> [L16] - Tradelines not sorted oldest → newest
> [L17] - Utilization Tier on non-revolving account
> [L18] - Creditor Name equals consumer name
> [L19] - Output contains narration
> [L20] - Any fabricated value
> # 【9†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-VNv4FPvydFUU21gNPYLp4Z】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn5file9
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v1.6_FAST
> [L3] 
> [L4] MISSION
> [L5] Fast, copy/paste-ready TSV extraction with fail-safe hard stops only.
> [L6] 
> [L7] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST → LOWEST)
> [L8] 1. SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS
> [L9] 2. MASTER_INDEX
> [L10] 3. SPEC_CORE
> [L11] 4. QA_AND_GATES
> [L12] 5. COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST
> [L13] 
> [L14] AUTHORITY CONTRACT
> [L15] - SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS: identity + global output constraints only.
> [L16] - MASTER_INDEX: execution flow + precedence.
> [L17] - SPEC_CORE: schema + allowed mappings + derived fields policy.
> [L18] - QA_AND_GATES: hard fails only (fail-safe).
> [L19] - COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST: absolute prohibitions.
> [L20] 
> [L21] EXECUTION FLOW (FAST; NO NARRATION)
> [L22] 
> [L23] PASS A — QUICK PARSE
> [L24] - Detect bureau (TU/EX/EQ) if available; else blank.
> [L25] - Detect report generated date if clearly labeled; else blank.
> [L26] - Extract tradelines into rows using section/table boundaries.
> [L27] 
> [L28] If the PDF is scanned/unreadable OR rows are fragmented → HARD FAIL (QA_AND_GATES) and STOP.
> [L29] 
> [L30] PASS B — BUILD TSV
> [L31] - Populate columns in SPEC_CORE schema.
> [L32] - If a field is not explicitly present, leave blank.
> [L33] - Apply utilization tier ONLY when Account Type is REVOLVING AND Credit Limit present AND Balance present.
> [L34] - Sort rows by Opening Date oldest→newest ONLY if ≥80% of rows have a parseable Opening Date; otherwise keep source order and add flag OPEN_DATE_SORT_SKIPPED.
> [L35] 
> [L36] PASS C — FINAL HARD QA
> [L37] - Enforce: schema exact, no narration, no fabricated placeholders.
> [L38] - If any hard check fails → QA FAIL and STOP.
> [L39] - Otherwise output TSV only.
> # 【10†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-5Q6MTFtuFmiaz5EFHTuNu2】

> [L4] You've shown recent use of credit cards and/or bank-issued open-ended accounts.
> [L5] FICO® Scores evaluate the mix of credit cards, installment loans and mortgages. People who demonstrate recent and responsible use of credit cards
> [L6] and/or bank-issued open-ended accounts are generally considered less risky to lenders.
> [L7] What's hurting
> [L8]  Short revolving history
> [L9] You have not established a long revolving and/or open-ended account credit history.
> [L10] Your ,rst revolving and/or open-ended account was opened: 6 Years, 11 Months ago
> [L11] FICO High Achievers opened their ,rst revolving and/or open-ended account 25 years ago, on average.
> [L12] People with longer credit histories who infrequently open new accounts generally pose less risk to lenders. In your case, the age of your oldest revolving
> [L13] and/or open-ended account and/or the average age of your revolving and/or open-ended accounts is relatively low. <PARSED TEXT FOR PAGE: 53 / 53> Experian 12/17/25, 3:06 PM

> [L16] Disclaimer
> [L17] About your FICO Score 8 or other FICO Scores
> [L18] Your FICO Score 8 powered by Experian data is formulated using the information in your credit ,le at the time it is requested. Many but not all
> [L19] lenders use FICO Score 8. In addition to the FICO Score 8, we may offer and provide other base or industry-speci,c FICO Scores (such as
> [L20] FICO Auto Scores and FICO Bankcard Scores). The other FICO Scores made available are calculated from versions of the base and
> [L21] industry-speci,c FICO Score models.
> [L22] Base FICO Scores (including the FICO Score 8) range from 300 to 850. Industry-speci,c FICO Scores range from 250-900. Higher scores
> [L23] represent a greater likelihood that you'll pay back your debts so you are viewed as being a lower credit risk to lenders. A lower FICO Score
> [L24] indicates to lenders that you may be a higher credit risk. There are many scoring models used in the marketplace. The type of score used, and
> [L25] its associated risk levels, may vary from lender to lender. But regardless of what scoring model is used, they all have one purpose: to
> [L26] summarize your creditworthiness. Keep in mind that your score is just one factor used in the application process. Other factors, such as your
> [L27] annual salary and length of employment, may also be considered by lenders when you apply for a loan.
> [L28] What this means to you:
> [L29] Credit scoring can help you understand your overall credit rating and help companies better understand how to serve you. Overall bene,ts of
> [L30] credit scoring have included faster credit approvals, reduction in human error and bias, consistency, and better terms and rates for American
> [L31] consumers through reduced costs and losses for lenders. Your lender or insurer may use a different FICO Score than FICO Score 8 or other
> [L32] base or industry-speci,c FICO Scores provided by us, or different scoring models to determine how you score.
